<script id="kb-r2">
/* KBR2 · WebGL2 renderer (Beta 3D 3.0 · Ultra Realistic Graphics Overhaul) */
(()=>{'use strict';
/* ===== KBR2 · A: matematika + proceduralne PBR teksture (bez spoljnih fajlova) ===== */
const TAU=Math.PI*2,clamp=(v,a,b)=>v<a?a:v>b?b:v,lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t,smooth=t=>t*t*(3-2*t);
const M4={
  // iste konvencije kao pogon: kolona-major, desna ruka, y gore, vertikalni FOV u radijanima
  persp(fov,asp,n,f){const v=1/Math.tan(fov/2),d=1/(n-f);return new Float32Array([v/asp,0,0,0,0,v,0,0,0,0,(f+n)*d,-1,0,0,2*f*n*d,0])},
  look(e,t,roll=0){let z=[e[0]-t[0],e[1]-t[1],e[2]-t[2]],l=Math.hypot(...z)||1;z=z.map(q=>q/l);let x=[z[2],0,-z[0]];l=Math.hypot(...x)||1;x=x.map(q=>q/l);let y=[z[1]*x[2]-z[2]*x[1],z[2]*x[0]-z[0]*x[2],z[0]*x[1]-z[1]*x[0]];const old=x;x=x.map((v,i)=>v*Math.cos(roll)+y[i]*Math.sin(roll));y=y.map((v,i)=>v*Math.cos(roll)-old[i]*Math.sin(roll));return new Float32Array([x[0],y[0],z[0],0,x[1],y[1],z[1],0,x[2],y[2],z[2],0,-x[0]*e[0]-x[1]*e[1]-x[2]*e[2],-y[0]*e[0]-y[1]*e[1]-y[2]*e[2],-z[0]*e[0]-z[1]*e[1]-z[2]*e[2],1])},
  mul(a,b){const o=new Float32Array(16);for(let c=0;c<4;c++)for(let r=0;r<4;r++)for(let k=0;k<4;k++)o[4*c+r]+=a[4*k+r]*b[4*c+k];return o},
  ortho(l,r,b,t,n,f){const w=1/(r-l),h=1/(t-b),d=1/(f-n);return new Float32Array([2*w,0,0,0,0,2*h,0,0,0,0,-2*d,0,-(r+l)*w,-(t+b)*h,-(f+n)*d,1])},
  lookDir(e,dir){let z=[-dir[0],-dir[1],-dir[2]];const up=Math.abs(dir[1])>.97?[1,0,0]:[0,1,0];let x=[up[1]*z[2]-up[2]*z[1],up[2]*z[0]-up[0]*z[2],up[0]*z[1]-up[1]*z[0]];let l=Math.hypot(...x)||1;x=x.map(q=>q/l);const y=[z[1]*x[2]-z[2]*x[1],z[2]*x[0]-z[0]*x[2],z[0]*x[1]-z[1]*x[0]];return {m:new Float32Array([x[0],y[0],z[0],0,x[1],y[1],z[1],0,x[2],y[2],z[2],0,-(x[0]*e[0]+x[1]*e[1]+x[2]*e[2]),-(y[0]*e[0]+y[1]*e[1]+y[2]*e[2]),-(z[0]*e[0]+z[1]*e[1]+z[2]*e[2]),1]),x,y,z}},
  planes(vp){const p=[];for(const [s,i] of [[1,0],[-1,0],[1,1],[-1,1],[1,2],[-1,2]]){const a=vp[3]+s*vp[i],b=vp[7]+s*vp[4+i],c=vp[11]+s*vp[8+i],d=vp[15]+s*vp[12+i],l=Math.hypot(a,b,c)||1;p.push([a/l,b/l,c/l,d/l])}return p}
};

M4.inv=function(m){const a=m,o=new Float32Array(16);
  const a00=a[0],a01=a[1],a02=a[2],a03=a[3],a10=a[4],a11=a[5],a12=a[6],a13=a[7],a20=a[8],a21=a[9],a22=a[10],a23=a[11],a30=a[12],a31=a[13],a32=a[14],a33=a[15];
  const b00=a00*a11-a01*a10,b01=a00*a12-a02*a10,b02=a00*a13-a03*a10,b03=a01*a12-a02*a11,b04=a01*a13-a03*a11,b05=a02*a13-a03*a12,b06=a20*a31-a21*a30,b07=a20*a32-a22*a30,b08=a20*a33-a23*a30,b09=a21*a32-a22*a31,b10=a21*a33-a23*a31,b11=a22*a33-a23*a32;
  let d=b00*b11-b01*b10+b02*b09+b03*b08-b04*b07+b05*b06;if(!d)return o;d=1/d;
  o[0]=(a11*b11-a12*b10+a13*b09)*d;o[1]=(a02*b10-a01*b11-a03*b09)*d;o[2]=(a31*b05-a32*b04+a33*b03)*d;o[3]=(a22*b04-a21*b05-a23*b03)*d;
  o[4]=(a12*b08-a10*b11-a13*b07)*d;o[5]=(a00*b11-a02*b08+a03*b07)*d;o[6]=(a32*b02-a30*b05-a33*b01)*d;o[7]=(a20*b05-a22*b02+a23*b01)*d;
  o[8]=(a10*b10-a11*b08+a13*b06)*d;o[9]=(a01*b08-a00*b10-a03*b06)*d;o[10]=(a30*b04-a31*b02+a33*b00)*d;o[11]=(a21*b02-a20*b04-a23*b00)*d;
  o[12]=(a11*b07-a10*b09-a12*b06)*d;o[13]=(a00*b09-a01*b07+a02*b06)*d;o[14]=(a31*b01-a30*b03-a32*b00)*d;o[15]=(a20*b03-a21*b01+a22*b00)*d;return o};
function aabbVisible(pl,mn,mx){for(const p of pl){const x=p[0]>0?mx[0]:mn[0],y=p[1]>0?mx[1]:mn[1],z=p[2]>0?mx[2]:mn[2];if(p[0]*x+p[1]*y+p[2]*z+p[3]<0)return false}return true}

/* --- brzi tileable proceduralni šum (optimizovan za 512x512 teksture) --- */
function rng(seed){let s=seed>>>0||1;return()=>{s^=s<<13;s>>>=0;s^=s>>>17;s^=s<<5;s>>>=0;return s/4294967296}}
function valueNoise(N,px,py,seed){
  const r=rng(seed),g=new Float32Array(px*py);for(let i=0;i<g.length;i++)g[i]=r();const o=new Float32Array(N*N);
  const xaA=new Int32Array(N),xbA=new Int32Array(N),txA=new Float32Array(N);
  for(let x=0;x<N;x++){const fx=x/N*px,x0=fx|0;xaA[x]=x0%px;xbA[x]=(x0+1)%px;const t=fx-x0;txA[x]=t*t*(3-2*t)}
  for(let y=0;y<N;y++){const fy=y/N*py,y0=fy|0,t=fy-y0,ty=t*t*(3-2*t),ya=(y0%py)*px,yb=((y0+1)%py)*px,row=y*N;
    for(let x=0;x<N;x++){const xa=xaA[x],xb=xbA[x],tx=txA[x];
      const a0=g[ya+xa],a1=g[ya+xb],b0=g[yb+xa],b1=g[yb+xb];
      const c0=a0+(a1-a0)*tx,c1=b0+(b1-b0)*tx;o[row+x]=c0+(c1-c0)*ty}}return o}
function fbm(N,px,py,oct,seed,gain=.5){const o=new Float32Array(N*N);let amp=1,sum=0;for(let k=0;k<oct;k++){const n=valueNoise(N,px<<k,py<<k,seed+k*977);for(let i=0;i<o.length;i++)o[i]+=n[i]*amp;sum+=amp;amp*=gain}const inv=1/sum;for(let i=0;i<o.length;i++)o[i]*=inv;return o}
function worley(N,cells,seed){
  const r=rng(seed),px=new Float32Array(cells*cells),py=new Float32Array(cells*cells);
  for(let i=0;i<cells*cells;i++){px[i]=r();py[i]=r()}
  const f1=new Float32Array(N*N),f2=new Float32Array(N*N),id=new Float32Array(N*N),invC=1/(cells*cells),sc=cells/N;
  for(let y=0;y<N;y++){const v=y*sc,cy=v|0,row=y*N;
    for(let x=0;x<N;x++){const u=x*sc,cx=u|0;let a=99,b=99,ai=0;
      for(let j=-1;j<=1;j++){const gy=((cy+j)%cells+cells)%cells,gyc=gy*cells,oy=cy+j-v;
        for(let i=-1;i<=1;i++){const gx=((cx+i)%cells+cells)%cells,idx=gyc+gx,dx=cx+i+px[idx]-u,dy=oy+py[idx],d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
          if(d2<a){b=a;a=d2;ai=idx}else if(d2<b)b=d2}}
      f1[row+x]=Math.sqrt(a);f2[row+x]=Math.sqrt(b);id[row+x]=ai*invC}}return {f1,f2,id}}
function hash01(n){const s=Math.sin(n*127.1+311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)}

/* --- 8 PBR materijala: alb(RGB detalj, A visina) + nor(RGB Scharr normala, A mikro-hrapavost/AO) --- */
const TEX_LAYERS=['malter','cigla','crep','kora','asfalt','trava','sljunak','blato'];
function buildMaterials(N){
  const L=8,alb=new Uint8Array(N*N*4*L),nor=new Uint8Array(N*N*4*L);
  const put=(layer,fn)=>{ // fn(x,y) -> [r,g,b,h,roughMod]
    const h=new Float32Array(N*N),rm=new Float32Array(N*N),base=layer*N*N*4;
    for(let y=0;y<N;y++)for(let x=0;x<N;x++){const v=fn(x,y),i=base+(y*N+x)*4;
      alb[i]=clamp(v[0],0,1)*255;alb[i+1]=clamp(v[1],0,1)*255;alb[i+2]=clamp(v[2],0,1)*255;alb[i+3]=clamp(v[3],0,1)*255;
      h[y*N+x]=v[3];rm[y*N+x]=v.length>4?clamp(v[4],0,1):.5}
    return {h,rm}};
  // Scharr 3x3 filter za ciste, glatke normal mape bez artefakata
  const normals=(layer,pack,str)=>{const h=pack.h,rm=pack.rm,base=layer*N*N*4,w=N;
    for(let y=0;y<N;y++){const ym=((y+w-1)%w)*w,y0=y*w,yp=((y+1)%w)*w;
      for(let x=0;x<N;x++){const xm=(x+w-1)%w,xp=(x+1)%w;
        const gx=(3*h[ym+xm]+10*h[y0+xm]+3*h[yp+xm]-3*h[ym+xp]-10*h[y0+xp]-3*h[yp+xp])*(str/16);
        const gy=(3*h[ym+xm]+10*h[ym+x]+3*h[ym+xp]-3*h[yp+xm]-10*h[yp+x]-3*h[yp+xp])*(str/16);
        let nx=gx,ny=gy,nz=1,l=Math.hypot(nx,ny,nz)||1;nx/=l;ny/=l;nz/=l;
        const i=base+(y0+x)*4;nor[i]=(nx*.5+.5)*255;nor[i+1]=(ny*.5+.5)*255;nor[i+2]=(nz*.5+.5)*255;nor[i+3]=rm[y0+x]*255}}};
  // 0 malter (srpski bavalit/fasadni malter + slivanje kise + mikropore)
  {const a=fbm(N,6,6,4,11),b=fbm(N,20,3,3,12),c=fbm(N,80,80,3,13),d=valueNoise(N,160,160,14);
    const p=put(0,(x,y)=>{const i=y*N+x,grain=(c[i]-.5)*.14+(d[i]-.5)*.08,v=.5+(a[i]-.5)*.22+(b[i]-.5)*.17+grain;
      const h=clamp(c[i]*.52+a[i]*.32+d[i]*.16,0,1);return [v,v*.998,v*.985,h,.62+(1-h)*.28]});normals(0,p,2.8)}
  // 1 cigla (terakota opeka sa dubokim fugama, oborenim ivicama i razlicitim pecenjem)
  {const rows=16,cols=4,bh=N/rows,bw=N/cols,gr=fbm(N,64,64,3,21),wth=fbm(N,12,12,3,22);
    const p=put(1,(x,y)=>{const row=Math.floor(y/bh),off=(row&1)*bw/2,xx=(x+off)%N,col=Math.floor(xx/bw),fx=(xx%bw)/bw,fy=(y%bh)/bh,id=row*7+col*13;
      const gx=Math.min(fx,1-fx)*bw,gy=Math.min(fy,1-fy)*bh,edge=Math.min(gx,gy*1.55),mW=N*(2.1/256),bW=N*(5.2/256);
      const mort=edge<mW?1:0,bev=clamp((edge-mW)/bW,0,1),t=hash01(id)*.24-.12,g=gr[y*N+x],w=wth[y*N+x];
      const chip=edge<mW*2.2?(g-.5)*.22:0;
      const v=mort?.40+g*.12:.54+t+(g-.5)*.16+(w-.5)*.10+chip;
      const h=mort?.06+g*.06:clamp(.52+bev*.38+(g-.5)*.12,0,1);
      return [v*(mort?.98:1.08),v*(mort?.99:.94),v*(mort?.97:.86),h,mort?.92:.58+(1-bev)*.25]});normals(1,p,4.2)}
  // 2 crep (balkanski falcovani crep: talasasti profil, preklop, mahovina u zlebovima)
  {const rows=8,cols=6,th=N/rows,tw=N/cols,mo=fbm(N,16,16,3,31),gr=fbm(N,96,96,2,32);
    const p=put(2,(x,y)=>{const row=Math.floor(y/th),off=(row&1)*tw/2,xx=(x+off)%N,col=Math.floor(xx/tw),fx=(xx%tw)/tw,fy=(y%th)/th,id=row*11+col*5,t=hash01(id)*.24-.12;
      const wave=.5+.5*Math.cos(fx*TAU),ridge=Math.abs(fx-.5)*2,lap=Math.pow(1-fy,2.4)*.55,side=smooth(clamp((ridge-.80)/.20,0,1));
      const m=mo[y*N+x]>.64?(mo[y*N+x]-.64)*1.9:0;
      const v=.50+t+(gr[y*N+x]-.5)*.14-side*.24-lap*.18+(wave-.5)*.08;
      const h=clamp(.22+fy*.42+wave*.26*(1-side)-lap*.22,0,1);
      return [v*(1-m*.42),v*(1+m*.22),v*(1-m*.52),h,clamp(.42+m*.45+side*.28,0,1)]});normals(2,p,3.8)}
  // 3 kora/drvo (duboke uzduzne brazde i pukotine kore)
  {const a=fbm(N,12,2,5,41,.56),b=fbm(N,5,24,3,42),c=valueNoise(N,96,32,43);
    const p=put(3,(x,y)=>{const i=y*N+x,r=a[i],cr=smooth(clamp((r-.28)/.35,0,1)),v=.34+cr*.48+(b[i]-.5)*.14+(c[i]-.5)*.08;
      return [v*1.03,v*.93,v*.80,clamp(cr*.82+c[i]*.18,0,1),.84-.18*cr]});normals(3,p,4.4)}
  // 4 asfalt (realistican srpski asfalt: agregat, habanje, katranske pukotine, varijacija sjaja)
  {const g1=valueNoise(N,160,160,51),g2=valueNoise(N,64,64,52),lo=fbm(N,6,6,4,53),w=worley(N,5,54),med=fbm(N,20,20,3,55);
    const p=put(4,(x,y)=>{const i=y*N+x,crack=clamp(1-(w.f2[i]-w.f1[i])*42,0,1)*smooth(clamp((lo[i]-.46)/.22,0,1));
      const patch=smooth(clamp((med[i]+lo[i]*.35-.78)/.09,0,1)), fleck=hash01(x+N*y);
      const sp=g1[i]>.88?.17:(g1[i]<.12?-.08:0);
      const wear=(lo[i]-.5)*.18+(med[i]-.5)*.10;
      const v=.5+(g1[i]-.5)*.18+(g2[i]-.5)*.14+wear+sp-crack*.34;
      const h=clamp(.52+(g1[i]-.5)*.34+(g2[i]-.5)*.18-crack*.46,0,1);
      const rm=clamp(.56+(g2[i]-.5)*.22+(lo[i]-.5)*.28-crack*.25,0,1);
      return [v*.99-patch*.07,v-patch*.072,v*1.02-patch*.068,clamp(h+patch*.07+(fleck-.5)*.09,0,1),clamp(rm-patch*.17,0,1)]});normals(4,p,2.3)}
  // 5 trava (gusti vlati + mikro senka korena)
  {const a=valueNoise(N,180,90,61),b=fbm(N,8,8,4,62),c=valueNoise(N,72,36,63);
    const p=put(5,(x,y)=>{const i=y*N+x,blade=a[i]*.55+c[i]*.45,v=.48+(blade-.5)*.44+(b[i]-.5)*.34;
      return [v*.93,v*1.06,v*.84,clamp(blade*.72+b[i]*.28,0,1),.78+(1-blade)*.18]});normals(5,p,2.0)}
  // 6 sljunak (ostri krecnjacki kamencici)
  {const w=worley(N,24,71),lo=fbm(N,8,8,3,72),fine=valueNoise(N,128,128,73);
    const p=put(6,(x,y)=>{const i=y*N+x,d=Math.min(1,w.f1[i]*1.65),st=hash01(w.id[i]*977)*.36-.18;
      const v=.56+st-d*.26+(lo[i]-.5)*.12+(fine[i]-.5)*.10;
      const h=clamp(1-d*d,0,1);return [v,v*.97,v*.91,h,.55+d*.38]});normals(6,p,4.4)}
  // 7 blato/zemlja (vlazna zemlja sa pukotinama i sitnim kamenjem)
  {const lo=fbm(N,5,5,4,81),mid=fbm(N,22,22,3,82),w=worley(N,7,83),gr=valueNoise(N,128,128,84);
    const p=put(7,(x,y)=>{const i=y*N+x,crack=clamp(1-(w.f2[i]-w.f1[i])*24,0,1)*.75;
      const v=.45+(lo[i]-.5)*.34+(mid[i]-.5)*.14+(gr[i]-.5)*.08-crack*.24;
      const h=clamp(lo[i]*.62+mid[i]*.28+gr[i]*.10-crack*.35,0,1);
      return [v*1.06,v*.96,v*.84,h,.66+(1-lo[i])*.24]});normals(7,p,2.8)}
  // normalizacija srednje svetline sloja = 0.5
  for(let layer=0;layer<L;layer++){const base=layer*N*N*4;let sum=0;for(let i=0;i<N*N;i++){const o=base+i*4;sum+=(alb[o]+alb[o+1]+alb[o+2])/3}const mean=sum/(N*N)/255,k=.5/Math.max(.05,mean);
    for(let i=0;i<N*N;i++){const o=base+i*4;alb[o]=clamp(alb[o]/255*k,0,1)*255;alb[o+1]=clamp(alb[o+1]/255*k,0,1)*255;alb[o+2]=clamp(alb[o+2]/255*k,0,1)*255}}
  return {alb,nor,N,L};
}

/* ===== KBR2 · B: GLSL ES 3.00 shaderi (PBR GGX + SSAO + Vogel PCF + Volumetrija + CAS) ===== */
const GLSL_HEAD='#version 300 es\nprecision highp float;precision highp int;precision highp sampler2D;precision highp sampler2DArray;precision highp sampler2DShadow;\n';
const GLSL_NOISE=`
float h11(float p){p=fract(p*.1031);p*=p+33.33;p*=p+p;return fract(p);}
float h21(vec2 p){vec3 q=fract(vec3(p.xyx)*.1031);q+=dot(q,q.yzx+33.33);return fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z);}
float h31(vec3 p){p=fract(p*.1031);p+=dot(p,p.zyx+31.32);return fract((p.x+p.y)*p.z);}
float ign(vec2 p){return fract(52.9829189*fract(dot(p,vec2(.06711056,.00583715))));}
float vnoise(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(h21(i),h21(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(h21(i+vec2(0,1)),h21(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}
float fbm2(vec2 p,int o){float a=.5,s=0.;for(int i=0;i<5;i++){if(i>=o)break;s+=a*vnoise(p);p=p*2.03+17.1;a*=.5;}return s;}
`;
/* nebo: Rayleigh/Mie atmosferski gradijent + gradski odsjaj Nisa nocu */
const GLSL_SKY=`
uniform vec3 uLDir;uniform vec3 uSunDir;uniform float uDusk;uniform float uRain;uniform float uFlash;uniform float uTime;uniform float uCloud;
vec3 skyBase(vec3 r){
  float ry=max(0.,r.y);
  vec3 horDay=pow(vec3(.74,.83,.89),vec3(2.2));
  vec3 zenDay=pow(vec3(.17,.40,.78),vec3(2.2));
  vec3 horNight=pow(vec3(.11,.155,.24),vec3(2.2));
  vec3 zenNight=pow(vec3(.018,.036,.085),vec3(2.2));
  vec3 hor=mix(horDay,horNight,uDusk);
  vec3 zen=mix(zenDay,zenNight,uDusk);
  vec3 c=mix(hor,zen,pow(ry,.46));
  // Diskretan topli odsjaj grada (Nis u dolini) blizu horizonta nocu
  float cityBand=exp(-ry*7.5)*uDusk*(1.-uRain*.35);
  float cityAz=.65+.35*sin(atan(r.x,r.z)*1.5+1.1);
  c+=vec3(.032,.020,.011)*cityBand*cityAz;
  // Olujno/kisno nebo
  vec3 grey=mix(pow(vec3(.50,.55,.60),vec3(2.2)),pow(vec3(.048,.060,.082),vec3(2.2)),uDusk);
  c=mix(c,grey,uRain*.76);
  // Sumrak/zora i Mie rasejanje oko sunca/meseca
  float s=max(dot(r,uSunDir),0.);
  float tw=(1.-smoothstep(-.08,.58,abs(uSunDir.y)));
  c+=vec3(1.,.46,.18)*pow(s,4.5)*tw*(1.-uRain*.82)*.72*(1.-uDusk*.55);
  c+=vec3(.95,.65,.40)*exp(-ry*5.5)*tw*(1.-uRain*.8)*.18*(1.-uDusk*.7);
  float m=max(dot(r,-uSunDir),0.);
  c+=vec3(.16,.24,.40)*pow(m,6.)*uDusk*(1.-uRain*.75)*.22;
  c+=vec3(.52,.66,.96)*(uFlash*.42);
  return c;
}`;

const VS_MAIN=GLSL_HEAD+`
layout(location=0) in vec3 aP;layout(location=1) in vec4 aC;layout(location=2) in float aT;layout(location=3) in vec2 aUV;layout(location=4) in vec4 aAux;layout(location=5) in vec3 aN;
uniform mat4 uVP;uniform vec3 uEye;out vec3 vP;out vec3 vC;out float vT;out vec2 vUV;flat out vec4 vAux;out vec3 vN;
invariant gl_Position;
void main(){vec3 P=aP;vT=aT;vC=aC.rgb;vP=aP;vUV=aUV*(1./16.);vAux=aAux;vN=aN;
  gl_Position=uVP*vec4(P,1.);}`;
const VS_PREPASS=GLSL_HEAD+`
layout(location=0) in vec3 aP;layout(location=1) in vec4 aC;layout(location=2) in float aT;
uniform mat4 uVP;uniform vec3 uEye;out vec3 vP;out vec3 vC;out float vT;
invariant gl_Position;
void main(){vec3 P=aP;vT=aT;vC=aC.rgb;vP=aP;
  gl_Position=uVP*vec4(P,1.);}`;
const VS_SHADOW=GLSL_HEAD+`
layout(location=0) in vec3 aP;layout(location=1) in vec4 aC;layout(location=2) in float aT;
uniform mat4 uVP;out vec3 vP;out vec3 vC;out float vT;
void main(){vP=aP;vC=aC.rgb;vT=aT;gl_Position=uVP*vec4(aP,1.);}`;
const VS_WIRE=GLSL_HEAD+`layout(location=0) in vec3 aP;uniform mat4 uVP;out vec3 vP;void main(){vP=aP;gl_Position=uVP*vec4(aP,1.);}`;
const FS_WIRE=GLSL_HEAD+GLSL_NOISE+GLSL_SKY+`
in vec3 vP;layout(location=0) out vec4 oC;uniform vec3 uEye;uniform vec2 uFog;uniform float uFogDens;uniform float uGroundY;uniform vec3 uSkyAmb;uniform vec3 uLCol;
void main(){vec3 Vv=uEye-vP;float dist=length(Vv);vec3 V=Vv/max(dist,.001);
  vec3 col=vec3(.042,.047,.052)*(.45+uSkyAmb.x+uSkyAmb.y+uSkyAmb.z+uLCol.x*.25);
  vec3 fogDirV=normalize(vec3(-V.x,max(.06,-V.y*.4),-V.z));vec3 fogCol=skyBase(fogDirV);
  float f1=smoothstep(uFog.x,uFog.y,dist)*.9,f2=1.-exp(-dist*uFogDens*exp(-max(vP.y-uGroundY,0.)*.042));
  col=mix(col,fogCol,clamp(max(f1,f2),0.,.97));oC=vec4(col,0.);}`;
const FS_DEPTH=GLSL_HEAD+`
in vec3 vP;in vec3 vC;in float vT;
void main(){if(vT>7.0&&vT<8.0)discard;}`;

/* ===== SSAO (depth-based hemisphere occlusion + edge-preserving bilateral blur) ===== */
const FS_SSAO=(def)=>GLSL_HEAD+def+GLSL_NOISE+`
in vec2 uv;layout(location=0) out vec4 oC;
uniform sampler2D uDepth;uniform mat4 uInvP;uniform mat4 uProj;
uniform float uRadius;uniform float uBias;
#ifdef CONTACT
uniform vec3 uViewLight;
#endif
vec3 viewPos(vec2 tc){
  float z=texture(uDepth,tc).r;
  vec4 p=uInvP*vec4(tc*2.-1.,z*2.-1.,1.);
  return p.xyz/p.w;
}
void main(){
  float d0=texture(uDepth,uv).r;
  if(d0>=.9999){oC=vec4(1.,0.,1.,1.);return;}
  vec3 P=viewPos(uv);
  float linKey=exp(-max(-P.z,0.)*.03);
  if(-P.z>150.){oC=vec4(1.,linKey,1.,1.);return;}
  vec3 N=normalize(cross(dFdx(P),dFdy(P)));
  if(dot(N,-P)<0.)N=-N;
  vec3 T=normalize(abs(N.y)<.9?cross(N,vec3(0.,1.,0.)):cross(N,vec3(1.,0.,0.)));
  vec3 B=cross(N,T);
  float ang=ign(gl_FragCoord.xy)*6.2831853;
  float rad=uRadius*clamp(-P.z*.045,.55,2.4);
  float occ=0.;
  for(int i=0;i<SSAO_TAPS;i++){
    float fi=(float(i)+.5)/float(SSAO_TAPS);
    float r=sqrt(fi)*rad;
    float a=float(i)*2.3999632+ang;
    float hz=sqrt(max(.04,1.-fi))*.88+.12;
    vec3 sp=P+(T*cos(a)+B*sin(a))*r+N*(hz*rad*.85);
    vec4 q=uProj*vec4(sp,1.);
    vec2 suv=q.xy/q.w*.5+.5;
    if(suv.x<=.002||suv.x>=.998||suv.y<=.002||suv.y>=.998)continue;
    float sz=viewPos(suv).z;
    float diff=abs(P.z-sz);
    float rc=(1.-smoothstep(rad*.2,rad*2.4,diff));
    occ+=step(sp.z+uBias,sz)*rc;
  }
  float fade=(1.-smoothstep(95.,145.,-P.z));
  float ao=mix(1.,pow(clamp(1.-occ/float(SSAO_TAPS)*1.55,0.,1.),1.25),fade);
  float contact=1.;
#ifdef CONTACT
  if(-P.z<62.&&dot(N,uViewLight)>.04){
    for(int j=1;j<=9;j++){
      float t=(float(j)-.48)*.125;vec3 sp=P+N*.042+uViewLight*t;
      vec4 clip=uProj*vec4(sp,1.);if(clip.w<=0.)break;
      vec2 tc=clip.xy/clip.w*.5+.5;if(any(lessThan(tc,vec2(0)))||any(greaterThan(tc,vec2(1))))break;
      float dz=viewPos(tc).z-sp.z;
      if(dz>.021+t*.012&&dz<.12+t*.16){contact=mix(.23,1.,t/1.15);break;}
    }
  }
#endif
  oC=vec4(ao,linKey,contact,1.);
}`;

const FS_SSAO_BLUR=GLSL_HEAD+`
in vec2 uv;layout(location=0) out vec4 oC;
uniform sampler2D uSrc;uniform vec2 uDir;
void main(){
  vec2 c0=texture(uSrc,uv).rg;
  float sum=c0.r*.26,wsum=.26;
  const float W[4]=float[4](.21,.15,.09,.04);
  for(int i=1;i<=4;i++){
    float wi=W[i-1];
    vec2 s1=texture(uSrc,uv+uDir*float(i)).rg;
    vec2 s2=texture(uSrc,uv-uDir*float(i)).rg;
    float k1=exp(-abs(s1.g-c0.g)*45.)*wi;
    float k2=exp(-abs(s2.g-c0.g)*45.)*wi;
    sum+=s1.r*k1+s2.r*k2;wsum+=k1+k2;
  }
  oC=vec4(sum/max(wsum,.001),c0.g,texture(uSrc,uv).b,1.);
}`;

/* ===== GLAVNI PBR FRAGMENT SHADER ===== */
const GLSL_LEAF=`
float leafCoverage(vec2 uv){
  vec2 g=uv*vec2(6.,4.);float row=floor(g.y),col=floor(g.x+mod(row,2.)*.5);
  vec2 p=vec2(fract(g.x+mod(row,2.)*.5)-.5,fract(g.y)-.5);
  p.x+=p.y*(mod(col+row,2.)-.5)*.9;
  float blade=(1.-abs(p.y)*1.85)*.47-abs(p.x);
  float stem=.027-abs(uv.x-.5+.09*sin(uv.y*5.));
  float envelope=1.-dot((uv-.5)*vec2(1.84,1.72),(uv-.5)*vec2(1.84,1.72));
  return step(0.,min(envelope,max(stem,blade)));
}`;
const FS_MAIN=(def)=>GLSL_HEAD+def+GLSL_NOISE+GLSL_SKY+GLSL_LEAF+`
in vec3 vP;in vec3 vC;in float vT;in vec2 vUV;flat in vec4 vAux;in vec3 vN;
layout(location=0) out vec4 oC;
uniform vec3 uEye;uniform vec3 uLCol;uniform vec3 uSkyAmb;uniform vec3 uGndAmb;
uniform vec2 uFog;uniform float uFogDens;uniform float uGroundY;uniform float uSeason;uniform float uTorch;uniform float uMorse;
uniform vec3 uTorchPos;uniform vec3 uTorchDir;uniform vec2 uBeam;uniform vec3 uLights[12];uniform vec3 uLightRGB[12];uniform vec4 uLobe[12];uniform vec4 uWindows[2];uniform vec3 uSignal;uniform vec3 uFlashPos;
#ifdef SUN_SHADOW
uniform mat4 uSunVP[2];uniform vec2 uCasc;uniform float uCascN;uniform vec2 uShadowTexel;uniform sampler2DShadow uSunShadow;
#endif
#ifdef SPOT_SHADOW
uniform mat4 uSpotVP;uniform sampler2DShadow uSpotShadow;uniform float uSpotOn;uniform vec2 uSpotTexel;
#endif
uniform sampler2DArray uTexA;uniform sampler2DArray uTexN;uniform float uTexOn;
#ifdef SSAO
uniform sampler2D uAO;uniform vec2 uInvRes;uniform float uAOOn;
#endif
uniform float uWet;uniform float uSnow;uniform float uInterior;
uniform sampler2D uTerrN;uniform sampler2D uTerrM;uniform vec3 uTerrBox;uniform float uTerrOn;uniform sampler2D uSign;
vec3 toLin(vec3 c){return pow(max(c,vec3(0.)),vec3(2.2));}

/* Cook-Torrance GGX PBR specular */
vec3 ggxSpec(vec3 N,vec3 V,vec3 L,float rough,vec3 F0){
  float ndl=max(dot(N,L),0.);
  if(ndl<=.0001)return vec3(0.);
  vec3 H=normalize(L+V);
  float ndv=max(dot(N,V),.001),ndh=max(dot(N,H),0.),vdh=max(dot(V,H),0.);
  float a=max(rough*rough,.022),a2=a*a;
  float den=ndh*ndh*(a2-1.)+1.;
  float D=a2/(3.14159265*den*den);
  float k=(rough+1.)*(rough+1.)*.125;
  float G=(ndv/(ndv*(1.-k)+k))*(ndl/(ndl*(1.-k)+k));
  vec3 F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(clamp(1.-vdh,0.,1.),5.);
  return (D*G*F)/max(4.*ndv*ndl,.001)*ndl;
}

#ifdef SUN_SHADOW
float sampleCascade(int c,vec3 P,vec3 N){
  float R=c==0?uCasc.x:uCasc.y;
  float texW=R*2.*uShadowTexel.y;
  vec3 Pn=P+N*(texW*1.65+.018);
  vec4 q=uSunVP[c]*vec4(Pn,1.);vec3 s=q.xyz*.5+.5;
  if(s.x<0.||s.x>1.||s.y<0.||s.y>1.||s.z>1.)return 1.;
  float m=2.5*uShadowTexel.x;
